id="u1a7f283e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">在磁盘 I/O </span><strong><span class="ne-text">操作完成后</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">才被触发；此时数据已传输完毕，CHS 地址早已使用过；</span></li><li id="u82387edb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">中断处理程序的作用是：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u45860628" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">读取状态寄存器，确认操作成功；</span></li><li id="u2d3bf6aa" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">唤醒等待 I/O 完成的进程；</span></li><li id="u1eace648" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">清除中断标志；</span></li></ul></ul><p id="u9c5f7cd9" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">📌</span></strong><strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 类比记忆：</span></strong></p><div class="ne-quote"><p id="u1328a2bd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">就像快递系统：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud5702d2c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">用户下单（用户程序）  </span></li><li id="u39ad59a2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">客服接收订单（系统调用）  </span></li><li id="ucd02f694" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">配送中心查地图规划路线（设备驱动 → CHS 转换）  </span></li><li id="ue2cef92d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">快递员送达后发短信通知（中断处理）</span></li></ul></div><p id="u492e11f2" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">只有配送中心知道“怎么走”——对应设备驱动负责寻址</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span><strong><span class="ne-text">本题选 C。</span></strong></p></details>


  
【2022-32】下列关于驱动程序的叙述中，**不正确的是**（ ）。  
A. 驱动程序与 I/O 控制方式无关  
B. 初始化设备是由驱动程序控制完成的  
C. 进程在执行驱动程序时可能进入阻塞态  
D. 读/写设备的操作是由驱动程序控制完成的  

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u05838c1d"><span class="ne-text">解析</span></summary><p id="u343cdc24" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">答案：A</span></strong></p><p id="u4dd4af13" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">A. 驱动程序与 I/O 控制方式无关 </span></strong><span class="ne-text">❌</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">错误</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> —— </span><strong><span class="ne-text">本题选 A</span></strong></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud0243bd9" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">I/O 控制方式</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 包括：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u80c8b5fc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">程序查询（Polling）</span></li><li id="udc7fe107" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">中断驱动（Interrupt-driven）</span></li><li id="ue4c67045" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">DMA（Direct Memory Access）</span></li><li id="ue961a102" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">通道控制（Channel）</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u4533453e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">驱动程序必须根据设备所采用的 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">I/O 控制方式</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 来设计其数据传输逻辑：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ubccc3b95" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">若设备使用 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">中断方式</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">，驱动需注册中断服务程序（ISR）；</span></li><li id="u71465adc" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">若使用 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">DMA</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">，驱动需配置 DMA 控制器的源/目标地址、传输长度；</span></li><li id="uc06fa526" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">若为 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">内存映射 I/O</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">，驱动通过内存访问指令读写设备寄存器；</span></li><li id="u9ea7a425" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">若为 </span><strong><span class="ne-text">端口映射 I/O</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">，则使用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">in</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">/</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">out</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 指令进行端口操作；</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u58faa243" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">因此，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">驱动程序的设计高度依赖于具体的 I/O 控制方式</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul><p id="u3695a404" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 核心要点：</span></strong></p><div data-type="success" class="ne-alert"><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u3c2b1a7b" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">驱动程序不是通用模块</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">，而是针对特定设备和 I/O 方式的定制化代码；</span></li><li id="u789ebf19" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">它必须知道设备如何通信（是轮询？中断？DMA？），才能正确实现数据传输；</span></li><li id="ua766775d" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">驱动程序是 I/O 控制方式的具体实现者</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">，二者密切相关，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">“与 I/O 控制方式无关”是严重误解</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul></div><hr id="Wo0Z3" class="ne-hr"><p id="ueb4dd0fc" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">B. 初始化设备是由驱动程序控制完成的 </span></strong><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 正确。</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u8ee6ad60" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">当系统启动或设备加载时，驱动程序负责：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u1ba716a1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">探测设备是否存在；</span></li><li id="u94d5c896" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">分配和映射 I/O 端口或内存；</span></li><li id="uadd1d4f4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">设置设备寄存器（如工作模式、波特率、DMA 使能等）；</span></li><li id="uec90a6fb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">启动设备并进入就绪状态；</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9f6a24c1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">这些都属于</span><strong><span class="ne-text">设备初始化</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">的核心任务。</span></li></ul><hr id="kBRji" class="ne-hr"><p id="u88896729" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">C. 进程在执行驱动程序时可能进入阻塞态 </span></strong><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 正确。</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uaf82601c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">驱动程序运行在内核态，但由用户进程触发（如调用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">read()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">）；</span></li><li id="ufefb2b33" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">在执行过程中，若设备尚未就绪（如磁盘未完成读取、缓冲区为空），驱动程序可调用：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="uf6021008" data-lake-index-type="0"><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">wait_event()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">、</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">sleep_on()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 等机制，使当前进程</span><strong><span class="ne-text">阻塞</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">；</span></li><li id="u8537b9ff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">直到设备产生中断、数据就绪后，由中断服务程序</span><strong><span class="ne-text">唤醒</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">该进程；</span></li></ul></ul><hr id="TKgQT" class="ne-hr"><p id="u861f4bda" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text">D. 读/写设备的操作是由驱动程序控制完成的 </span></strong><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 正确。</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uee681ac2" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">用户程序通过 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">read()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">、</span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">write()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 系统调用请求 I/O；</span></li><li id="ud209476c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">系统调用处理程序将请求转发给对应设备的驱动程序；</span></li><li id="u70da7a2f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">驱动程序负责：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u26d285ae" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">将逻辑请求转化为硬件操作；</span></li><li id="ubdf0de78" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">向设备寄存器写入命令；</span></li><li id="u9125a6bb" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">启动数据传输；</span></li><li id="u04a7eaff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">管理数据缓冲区；</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u77b259d9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">所有对设备的直接控制均由驱动完成。</span></li></ul></details>


【2023-46】（8分）  
进程P通过执行系统调用从键盘接收一个字符的输入。已知此过程中与进程P相关的操作包括：  
① 将进程P插入就绪队列  
② 将进程P插入阻塞队列  
③ 将字符从键盘控制器读入系统缓冲区  
④ 启动键盘中断处理程序  
⑤ 进程P从系统调用返回  
⑥ 用户在键盘上输入字符  

请回答下列问题。

**(1) 按照正确的操作顺序，操作①的前一个和后一个操作分别是上述操作中的哪一个？操作⑥的后一个操作是哪一个？**

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u7310ae97"><span class="ne-text">答案</span></summary><p id="u915a746c" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 正确操作顺序：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">② → ⑥ → ④ → ③ → ① → ⑤</span></strong></p><p id="uc3e441a8" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">详细流程说明：</span></strong></p><ol class="ne-ol"><li id="u1a63b0d3" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">② 将进程P插入阻塞队列</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：  </span></li></ol><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="ue6e012cf" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">进程P执行 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">read()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 等系统调用等待键盘输入，但输入尚未到达；</span></li><li id="u582e101c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">操作系统将其状态设为“阻塞”，并插入</span><strong><span class="ne-text">阻塞队列</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">（或等待队列）；</span></li><li id="u659c427b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">CPU 调度其他进程运行。</span></li></ul></ul><ol start="2" class="ne-ol"><li id="ub2e76ac3" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">⑥ 用户在键盘上输入字符</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：  </span></li></ol><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u5e44bc04" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">用户按下按键，键盘硬件产生键码。</span></li></ul></ul><ol start="3" class="ne-ol"><li id="ue9280e1c" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">④ 启动键盘中断处理程序</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：  </span></li></ol><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u6c0a50e9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">键盘控制器向CPU发出中断请求；</span></li><li id="u2cbd72db" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">CPU响应中断，切换到内核态，启动</span><strong><span class="ne-text">键盘中断处理程序</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul></ul><ol start="4" class="ne-ol"><li id="u71b1e05b" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">③ 将字符从键盘控制器读入系统缓冲区</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：  </span></li></ol><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="uaa1617a3" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">中断处理程序从I/O端口读取键码；</span></li><li id="ud985659e" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">存入</span><strong><span class="ne-text">内核缓冲区</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">；</span></li><li id="uf8e1fe08" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">属于键盘驱动程序的职责。</span></li></ul></ul><ol start="5" class="ne-ol"><li id="u6dad18a6" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">① 将进程P插入就绪队列</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：  </span></li></ol><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u66ff39b1" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">中断处理程序发现有进程（P）正在等待输入；</span></li><li id="u8a00f996" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">将其从</span><strong><span class="ne-text">阻塞队列</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">移出，插入</span><strong><span class="ne-text">就绪队列</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">；</span></li><li id="ud227012f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">表示进程P已具备继续执行的条件。</span></li></ul></ul><ol start="6" class="ne-ol"><li id="u72380ba4" data-lake-index-type="0"><strong><span class="ne-text">⑤ 进程P从系统调用返回</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：  </span></li></ol><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="u94ba0f80" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">当进程P被调度执行时，从系统调用的阻塞点恢复；</span></li><li id="u6876d6da" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">返回用户态，继续执行后续指令（如 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">printf</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 输出读到的字符）。</span></li></ul></ul><hr id="OcxEp" class="ne-hr"><p id="u86175580" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">答案：</span></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u865ff18c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">操作①的前一个操作是：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">③</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">  </span></li><li id="u6944cdf0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">操作①的后一个操作是：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">⑤</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">  </span></li><li id="u42060f72" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">操作⑥的后一个操作是：</span><strong><span class="ne-text">④</span></strong></li></ul></details>
---

**(2) 在上述哪个操作之后CPU一定从进程P切换到其他进程？在上述哪个操作之后CPU调度程序才能选择进程P执行？**

**第一问：CPU一定从进程P切换到其他进程的操作**

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u2693ee98"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">答案</span></summary><p id="u9f6a3a6b" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">② 将进程P插入阻塞队列</span></strong></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u9960bb75" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">当进程P因等待I/O而被阻塞时，其状态变为“阻塞”，不再参与调度；</span></li><li id="ub138f158" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">调度器必须选择另一个就绪进程运行；</span></li><li id="uf144eca0" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">因此，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">在②之后，CPU一定会发生进程切换</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul><p id="ue883cff7" class="ne-p"><strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">第二问：CPU调度程序才能选择进程P执行的操作</span></strong></p><p id="uc66c7ebd" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">① 将进程P插入就绪队列</span></strong></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uecb09b32" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">只有进入</span><strong><span class="ne-text">就绪队列</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">的进程才能被调度器选中；</span></li><li id="u4230884d" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">在①之前，P处于阻塞状态，不可调度；</span></li><li id="u259ea54f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">在①之后，P成为就绪态，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">可被调度执行</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul></details>
---

**(3) 完成上述哪个操作的代码属于键盘驱动程序？**

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="ud1ada311"><span class="ne-text">答案</span></summary><p id="u42ee0cb9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">③ 将字符从键盘控制器读入系统缓冲区</span></strong></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ub5403bff" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">该操作由</span><strong><span class="ne-text">键盘中断服务程序</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">完成；而</span><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: #DF2A3F; font-size: 13px">中断服务程序是</span></strong><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: #DF2A3F">键盘驱动程序的一部分</span></strong><strong><span class="ne-text" style="color: #DF2A3F; font-size: 13px">；</span></strong></li><li id="ua1e519b6" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">驱动程序负责：</span></li></ul><ul class="ne-list-wrap"><ul ne-level="1" class="ne-ul"><li id="uc0c77a4c" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">读取硬件寄存器；</span></li><li id="u25374471" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">解码键码；</span></li><li id="uac1820d4" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">存入内核输入缓冲区；</span></li></ul></ul><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="ud2b5813f" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">因此，③是驱动程序的核心功能。</span></li></ul><div class="ne-quote"><p id="ub8cb7ef9" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">扩展说明</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">：驱动程序还包括初始化、中断注册、错误处理等，但③是其在I/O过程中最关键的硬件交互操作。</span></p></div></details>
---

**(4) 键盘中断处理程序执行时，进程P处于什么状态？CPU处于内核态还是用户态？**

<details class="lake-collapse"><summary id="u9901e86e"><span class="ne-text">答案</span></summary><p id="u564c9fab" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">第一问：进程P的状态</span><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">阻塞态（或等待态）</span></strong></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="uc0a3fb77" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">在键盘中断发生前，进程P已因等待输入被</span><strong><span class="ne-text">主动阻塞</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">；</span></li><li id="ua973fec9" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">它当前不在运行，也不在就绪队列中；</span></li><li id="ue884f33b" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">正在等待“输入完成”这一事件；</span></li><li id="u405c0576" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">因此，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">当中断处理程序执行时，进程P仍处于阻塞状态</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul><div class="ne-quote"><p id="uace5ca98" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text">⚠️</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 注意：中断处理程序执行本身不会立即唤醒P，但会在处理完后调用 </span><code class="ne-code"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">wake_up()</span></code><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> 等机制唤醒。</span></p></div><p id="u7cf13b91" class="ne-p"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">第二问：CPU的状态</span><span class="ne-text">✅</span><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px"> </span><strong><span class="ne-text">内核态</span></strong></p><ul class="ne-ul"><li id="u2189a4cd" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">中断发生后，CPU自动切换到内核态；中断处理程序运行在内核空间；可访问内核数据结构、设备寄存器等特权资源；</span></li><li id="ue3193ef8" data-lake-index-type="0"><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">因此，</span><strong><span class="ne-text">CPU处于内核态</span></strong><span class="ne-text" style="font-size: 13px">。</span></li></ul></details>
 总结图示（流程时间线）：

```plain
用户程序: P 执行 read() → 阻塞
    ↓
② P 插入阻塞队列 → 调度其他进程
    ↓
⑥ 用户按键
    ↓
④ 触发中断 → 启动中断处理程序
    ↓
③ 驱动程序读键码 → 存入内核缓冲区
    ↓
① 唤醒P → 插入就绪队列
    ↓（调度）
⑤ P 被调度 → 从系统调用返回 → 继续执行
```
